Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The networks variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 96 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 938 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_96(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 96. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^938(S, F) is trivial. QED.
